# Canned corn

<section class="hmi-quick-read" id="canned-corn-quick-read" data-hmi-quick-read aria-labelledby="canned-corn-quick-read-label"><div class="hmi-quick-read-label" id="canned-corn-quick-read-label">Quick read</div>

Canned corn in the HMI library has 71 reported results for lead, cadmium, total arsenic, total mercury, nickel, chromium, aluminium, uranium, Co, Cu, Fe, Mn, Zn, V, Se, B, Ba, Ca, I, Mg, Mo and Sr from 8 sources.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug <ul>
<li>
<strong>Metals measured:</strong>

Lead, cadmium, total arsenic, total mercury, nickel and chromium and others, in 71 results from 8 sources.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Tokay F · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables">Determination of Some Elements in Canned and Frozen Vegetables Sold in Balıkesir (Türkiye) Markets and Nutritional Assessment</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Adıyaman University Journal of Science (ADYU J SCI) 12(2), 230-243</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
<li>
<strong>Main drivers:</strong>

Form, basis and where the canned corn was sampled; reporting limits decide how many results are non-detects.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Tokay F · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables">Determination of Some Elements in Canned and Frozen Vegetables Sold in Balıkesir (Türkiye) Markets and Nutritional Assessment</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Adıyaman University Journal of Science (ADYU J SCI) 12(2), 230-243</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
<li>
<strong>Practical framing:</strong>

Ask for lot results with a quantification limit well below the limit that applies in the destination market.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Tokay F · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables">Determination of Some Elements in Canned and Frozen Vegetables Sold in Balıkesir (Türkiye) Markets and Nutritional Assessment</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Adıyaman University Journal of Science (ADYU J SCI) 12(2), 230-243</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</li>
</ul>

from 8 sources.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span href="../sources/alsabagh2025-canned-products-baghdad-heavy-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/dabeka1995-canadian-food-composites-metals|6]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Dabeka RW, McKenzie AD · 1995</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/dabeka1995-canadian-food-composites-metals">Survey of Lead, Cadmium, Fluoride, Nickel, and Cobalt in Food Composites and Estimation of Dietary Intakes of These Elements by Canadians in 1986-1988</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Journal of AOAC International</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/dabeka1995-canadian-food-composites-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> WHO GEMS/Food submissions reported lead in canned corn, as sold from 141 results in 3 reporting regions, with subgroup medians below 45 µg/kg, mostly non-detects.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> In the US Total Diet Study, canned corn, canned had a median lead result below 4 µg/kg across 3 composites from 2018–2020.<sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

Dabeka et al. 1995 reported lead at 9.8 µg/kg (median) in canned corn, canned from 5 city composites.<sup class="cite">[[sources/dabeka1995-canadian-food-composites-metals|6]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Dabeka RW, McKenzie AD · 1995</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/dabeka1995-canadian-food-composites-metals">Survey of Lead, Cadmium, Fluoride, Nickel, and Cobalt in Food Composites and Estimation of Dietary Intakes of These Elements by Canadians in 1986-1988</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Journal of AOAC International</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/dabeka1995-canadian-food-composites-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> Spungen et al. 2024 reported cadmium at 1.3 µg/kg (mean) in canned corn, canned from all samples.<sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

For a buyer the practical points are the form bought, the basis a result is reported on, where the canned corn came from and the laboratory’s reporting limit. Testing finds contamination but does not remove it.

<div class="hmi-analyte-explorer" id="hmi-occurrence-canned-corn" data-analyte-explorer data-compact-intro="true" data-source-summaries="true"><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure" data-explorer-context><summary data-open-context aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-controls="hmi-occurrence-canned-corn-drawer">Comparison methods and scope</summary><div data-explorer-context-content>

<p>Each row is one statistic a source reports for one form of canned corn, group and metal; percentiles, ranges and maxima reported for the same samples as a mean or median are shown on that row. Different forms and preparation states are kept apart, and dry-weight results never share a reference with as-sold or fresh-weight results. Ratings place a result against an HMI reference built from monitoring results and ordinary-setting medians for the same form and basis. Same-study contrasts compare a contaminated or treated site with its control, targeted with random sampling, a prepared form with the unprocessed one, or one group of a study with the median of its other groups. Held rows failed a check against their own source and are shown but not compared.</p>

dataset methods and interpretation</summary><div data-shared-evidence-content><p>Source summaries retain their original statistic, material, basis and censoring. Each source drawer identifies methods and overlapping samples. No matched reference or pooled distribution is inferred.</p></div></details><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure" data-baseline-fallback id="hmi-reference-hmi-pooled-reference-v2:canned-corn-d593ed8ef124:7cd7d2dcbc93"><summary>HMI canned corn Pb reference · Canned corn, canned</summary><div data-baseline-reference-template="hmi-pooled-reference-v2:canned-corn-d593ed8ef124:7cd7d2dcbc93"><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Reference and scope</p><p>HMI canned corn Pb reference · Canned corn, canned. Canned corn, canned; As sold, as eaten or fresh weight, µg/kg; no moisture conversion. Distribution of subgroup median concentration values.</p><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Reference (µg/kg)</th><th scope="col">Q1</th><th scope="col">Median</th><th scope="col">Q3</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Primary HMI reference</td><td>≤4</td><td>≤4</td><td>≤110</td></tr><tr><td>Alternative weighting</td><td>≤4</td><td>≤4</td><td>≤110</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p>5 reference units; 3 sampling families. Counts describe evidence units, not independent studies or certainty.</p><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Contributing family</th><th scope="col">Reference units</th><th scope="col">Weight</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>FDA Total Diet Study</td><td>3</td><td>33%</td></tr><tr><td>Spungen et al. 2024</td><td>1</td><td>33%</td></tr><tr><td>Tokay 2022</td><td>1</td><td>33%</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Methods</p><p>HMI pools subgroup medians for canned corn, canned on a as sold, as eaten or fresh weight basis: monitoring results summarized as one median per reporting region, sampling type and collection year, total diet composites and printed sample values as single observations, and printed subgroup medians from ordinary farms or markets. Each sampling family has equal total weight. Non-detects stay as zero-to-limit intervals, so quartiles carry lower and upper bounds. Means, ranges and maxima never enter as observations. Other forms, other bases, targeted sampling and results from mining, industrial, wastewater-affected, experimental, fertiliser or manufacturer settings are kept out of this reference.</p><p>Inverse weighted empirical CDF; midpoint at exact cumulative-weight boundaries. At least four reference units and a definitely positive Q1 to Q3 spread are required. This is a display eligibility rule, not statistical validation.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Limitations</p><p>3 sampling families with different designs; none is a probability sample of world supply. Reporting regions and purchase markets are not growing areas. Non-detects with differing limits widen the bounds. A position is descriptive concentration, not a safety or compliance judgment.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Admitted source families</p><ul><li><a href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020" class="internal" data-slug="sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020" data-no-cite-meta>FDA Total Diet Study</a></li><li><a href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium" class="internal" data-slug="sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium" data-no-cite-meta>Spungen et al. 2024</a></li><li><a href="../sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables" class="internal" data-slug="sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables" data-no-cite-meta>Tokay 2022</a></li></ul><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure"><summary>Reference version and interpretation</summary><p>Frozen evidence selection: 2026-10-22. Method: hmi-pooled-reference-v2.</p><p>hmi-pooled-reference-v2:canned-corn-d593ed8ef124:7cd7d2dcbc93</p><p>The reference is computed by HMI from admitted evidence, not copied from a study baseline. It describes the evidence currently in this scope; it is not a worldwide or current-lot benchmark. Source, year and region filters do not change it. Different HMI scopes are not one common ranking.</p></details></div></details><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure" data-baseline-fallback id="hmi-reference-hmi-pooled-reference-v2:canned-corn-ed6d23b1b0b8:cc6c5bf4901b"><summary>HMI canned corn Pb reference · Canned corn, canned, dry weight</summary><div data-baseline-reference-template="hmi-pooled-reference-v2:canned-corn-ed6d23b1b0b8:cc6c5bf4901b"><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Reference and scope</p><p>HMI canned corn Pb reference · Canned corn, canned, dry weight. Canned corn, canned; Dry weight, µg/kg; no moisture conversion. Distribution of subgroup median concentration values.</p><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Reference (µg/kg)</th><th scope="col">Q1</th><th scope="col">Median</th><th scope="col">Q3</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Primary HMI reference</td><td>1200</td><td>1500</td><td>1800</td></tr><tr><td>Alternative weighting</td><td>1200</td><td>1500</td><td>1800</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p>2 reference units; 1 sampling family. Counts describe evidence units, not independent studies or certainty.</p><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Contributing family</th><th scope="col">Reference units</th><th scope="col">Weight</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Alsabagh 2025</td><td>2</td><td>100%</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Methods</p><p>HMI pools subgroup medians for canned corn, canned on a dry weight basis: monitoring results summarized as one median per reporting region, sampling type and collection year, total diet composites and printed sample values as single observations, and printed subgroup medians from ordinary farms or markets. Each sampling family has equal total weight. Non-detects stay as zero-to-limit intervals, so quartiles carry lower and upper bounds. Means, ranges and maxima never enter as observations. Other forms, other bases, targeted sampling and results from mining, industrial, wastewater-affected, experimental, fertiliser or manufacturer settings are kept out of this reference.</p><p>Inverse weighted empirical CDF; midpoint at exact cumulative-weight boundaries. At least four reference units and a definitely positive Q1 to Q3 spread are required. This is a display eligibility rule, not statistical validation.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Limitations</p><p>Alsabagh 2025 is the only compatible sampling family for canned corn, canned on this basis in the completed canned corn ledger, so this single-family reference cannot represent other markets. Basis: As sold, as eaten or fresh weight.</p><p>Non-detects are intervals from zero to the reporting limit; never a measured zero</p></div><div class="hmi-selected-comparison" data-selected-comparison><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">HMI baseline position</p><p>Limited data · HMI canned corn Pb reference · Canned corn, canned. median as reported: ≤4 µg/kg. HMI median ≤4; Q1 ≤4; Q3 ≤110 µg/kg. 3 families. Reporting bounds prevent a rating.</p><p>Reporting bounds or insufficient reference spread prevent a stable Low / Moderate / High classification.</p><p>This finding’s sampling family contributes to the fixed reference.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sensitivity to weighting</p><p>Equal weight per subgroup: Limited data. The position remains unresolved under this alternative. This check does not address all sampling, rounding or analytical uncertainty.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sensitivity to source selection</p><ul><li>Without FDA Total Diet Study: Limited data.</li><li>Without Spungen et al. 2024: Limited data.</li><li>Without Tokay 2022: Limited data.</li></ul><p>These checks show dependence on each sampling family; they are not confidence intervals.</p><div data-baseline-reference="hmi-pooled-reference-v2:canned-corn-d593ed8ef124:7cd7d2dcbc93"><a href="#hmi-reference-hmi-pooled-reference-v2:canned-corn-d593ed8ef124:7cd7d2dcbc93">Reference, contributing evidence and methods</a></div></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Collection period</p><p data-timeline-scope>2018–2020</p><div data-selected-timeline><p>No year-by-year sample register is reconstructed from this summary.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Geographic scope</p><p data-regional-scope>1 collection regions</p><div data-selected-regional><p>Purchase or reporting market; growing area not identified.</p></div><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Sampling, analysis and reporting limits</p><p>As described by the monitoring programme.</p><p>Non-detects are intervals from zero to the reporting limit; never a measured zero</p><p></p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Shared samples and study overlap</p><p>Subgroup rows from the same source share its laboratory and sampling frame; they are not independent studies.</p><p>Each table row describes a food/analyte result. Repeated rows do not add independent samples or datasets. No cross-study trend is calculated in this comparison mode.</p><details class="hmi-drawer-disclosure"><summary>Reported values, conversion and source locator</summary><div class="hmi-drawer-table"><table><thead><tr><th scope="col">Source statistic</th><th scope="col">Original result</th><th scope="col">Displayed unit conversion</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>median</td><td>0 of 3 detected; HMI median of individual results</td><td>as recorded in the findings store</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p>3 dataset rows, for example Original FDA TSV row 9659 (header = row 1).</p><p>No moisture, cooking-yield or species conversion is applied to the original finding above. Any basis conversion used for its HMI comparison is stated separately with the numerical target.</p></details><p><a href="https://www.fda.gov/food/fda-total-diet-study-tds/fda-total-diet-study-tds-results">Open the primary publication</a> · <a href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020" class="internal" data-slug="sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">HMI source record</a></p></div><div data-evidence-content><p class="hmi-dataset-title"><a href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium" class="internal" data-slug="sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Spungen et al. 2024</a></p><p>Canned corn, canned · Lead (Pb) · 3 samples.</p><p class="hmi-drawer-section-title" role="heading" aria-level="3">Selected finding and reference</p><div class="hmi-selected-result" data-selected-finding><strong class="hmi-finding">&lt;4 µg/kg</strong><span class="hmi-row-note">not detected.</span><p>3 samples. <a id="trade-offs-and-failure-modes"></a>
## Trade-offs and limits of controls

<a id="hmi-ing-022"></a>
<a id="can-an-intervention-increase-another-contaminant-or-redistribute-contamination-to-a-by-product"></a>
<a id="hmi-ing-023"></a>

<a id="hmi-ing-024"></a>
<a id="what-costs-constraints-adverse-effects-or-failure-modes-are-documented-and-which-are-unknown"></a>

Metals do not move together, so a lot that passes a lead limit says little about cadmium, arsenic or nickel unless those are tested as well.

References identify the sources used in these answers. The Sources index preserves additional discovery links; inclusion there does not mean a source has been reviewed for this assessment. Corrections, completed searches, new direct evidence and changes in requirements should reopen the relevant answer.


<a id="universal-faqs"></a>
## Canned corn FAQs

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="what-heavy-metals-are-found-in-canned-corn" data-anchor="what-heavy-metals-are-found-in-canned-corn" data-question="What heavy metals are found in canned corn?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>What heavy metals are found in canned corn?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

The library reports lead, cadmium, total arsenic, total mercury, nickel, chromium, aluminium, uranium, Co, Cu, Fe, Mn, Zn, V, Se, B, Ba, Ca, I, Mg, Mo and Sr in canned corn, most often lead, cadmium and total arsenic.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020|2]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">U.S. Food and Drug Administration · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">FY2018-FY2020 TDS Elements Analytical Results</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">FDA Total Diet Study</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/fda2022-tds-elements-fy2018-fy2020">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium|3]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Spungen JH, et al. · 2024</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Infants&#39; and young children&#39;s dietary exposures to lead and cadmium: FDA total diet study 2018-2020</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Food Additives &amp; Contaminants: Part A</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/spungen2024-fda-tds-infant-lead-cadmium">Read source →</a></span></span></sup><sup class="cite">[[sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables|4]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Tokay F · 2022</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables">Determination of Some Elements in Canned and Frozen Vegetables Sold in Balıkesir (Türkiye) Markets and Nutritional Assessment</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Adıyaman University Journal of Science (ADYU J SCI) 12(2), 230-243</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/tokay2022-balikesir-canned-frozen-vegetables">Read source →</a></span></span></sup> WHO GEMS/Food submissions reported lead in canned corn, as sold from 141 results in 3 reporting regions, with subgroup medians below 45 µg/kg, mostly non-detects.<sup class="cite">[[sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants|1]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">World Health Organization · 2026</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">GEMS/Food Contaminants database heavy-metal exports</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">GEMS/Food Contamination Monitoring and Assessment Programme</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/who-gemsfood-heavy-metal-contaminants">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>

<details class="qa" data-hmi-faq="1" id="which-origins-or-products-of-canned-corn-measured-lowest" data-anchor="which-origins-or-products-of-canned-corn-measured-lowest" data-question="Which origins or products of canned corn measured lowest?">
<summary><span class="mk" aria-hidden="true"></span>Which origins or products of canned corn measured lowest?</summary>
<div class="ans" data-hmi-faq-answer="1">

In one study, lead across 6 groups of canned corn ranged from 4.3 µg/kg (Halifax 1986) to 133 µg/kg (Winnipeg 1987); the lowest is the lowest observed in that study, not an established sourcing preference.<sup class="cite">[[sources/dabeka1995-canadian-food-composites-metals|6]]<span class="cite-card"><span class="cite-card-head"><span class="cite-card-authors">Dabeka RW, McKenzie AD · 1995</span><a class="cite-card-title" href="../sources/dabeka1995-canadian-food-composites-metals">Survey of Lead, Cadmium, Fluoride, Nickel, and Cobalt in Food Composites and Estimation of Dietary Intakes of These Elements by Canadians in 1986-1988</a></span><span class="cite-card-body"><span class="cite-card-journal">Journal of AOAC International</span><a class="cite-card-review" href="../sources/dabeka1995-canadian-food-composites-metals">Read source →</a></span></span></sup>

</div>
</details>
